## `AFG-9601-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Textile Shrinkage Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dry Cleaning Perc Residual Concentration & Fabric Dimensional Shrinkage
- **Intro:** Textile Shrinkage Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dry Cleaning Perc Residual Concentration & Fabric Dimensional Shrinkage — Pre-Shift Instrument Calibration Verification: Analyzer accuracy ±2 ppm at 50 ppm; thermometer accuracy ±0.5°C; pressure gauge accuracy ±0.1 bar; flow meter accuracy ±0.5 L/min.
- **Key tolerance:** Analyzer accuracy ±2 ppm at 50 ppm; thermometer accuracy ±0.5°C; pressure gauge accuracy ±0.1 bar; flow meter accuracy ±0.5 L/min.
- **Standards:** `AFS 2018-01`, `ISO 3175-4:2018`, `ISO 3759:2011`, `ANSI/IICRC S300:2015 Standard for Professional Dry Cleaning`
